# Inventoried UI strings the addon deliberately does not translate. One `KEY  # reason` per line.
# tests/python/test_ui_coverage.py: every key in pipeline/ui_inventory.txt is in ui_keys.txt or here (never both),
# and every line here has a reason. Regenerate the inventory with `python -m wfj.dev.ui_inventory` (see its docstring).
# The always-visible windows and help tooltips were triaged from the pinned UI source (docs/research/2026-09-14-whole-ui-window-inventory.md).
